### [1] [Trustworthy FinAInce: Unpacking How AI-Mediated Financial Advice is Judged](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20989)
*Aryan Ramchandra Kapadia,Eshwar Chandrasekharan,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过随机情境实验（285名美国成年人、8类财务决策）考察人们对生成式AI财务建议的评价机制，发现建议风格、来源标签与决策情境对评价有不同影响，评价进一步显著预测质量判断、信任与采纳意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI日益成为个人财务建议来源，理解人们如何评价此类建议，对于促进"恰当依赖"（而非盲目信任）十分关键。

Method: 采用随机情境实验（vignette），样本为285名美国成年人，覆盖8类财务决策；独立操纵三种建议风格（AI、专家、网络社区），在保持底层推荐内容一致的前提下显示来源标签。同时设置无来源标签条件以检验风格本身的效应。

Result: 建议风格对信息评价与安全评价影响最强；"专家"标签选择性提升对来源知识水平的感知；决策情境主要影响风险与安全评价。这些评价与下游判断相关，模型分别解释整体质量69.2%、信任75.9%、预期依赖82.9%。此外，即使不显示来源标签，专家风格建议仍最受偏好。

Conclusion: 研究有助于理解财务建议评价过程，区分建议风格与来源标签的独立作用，并为财务AI设计提供启示：应支持有依据的评价，而非单纯追求最大化信任。

Abstract: As generative AI is increasingly used as a source of personal financial guidance, understanding how people appraise such advice is important for supporting appropriate reliance. We conducted a randomized vignette experiment with 285 U.S. adults across eight financial decisions, independently varying three advice styles---AI, expert, and online community---and displayed source labels while holding the underlying recommendation consistent. Advice style most strongly shaped message and safety appraisals, Expert labels selectively increased perceived source knowledge, and decision context primarily shaped risk and safety appraisals. These appraisals were associated with downstream judgments, with models explaining 69.2% of overall quality, 75.9% of trust, and 82.9% of intended reliance. Expert-style advice also remained most preferred when shown without source labels. Our findings have implications for understanding financial advice evaluation, distinguishing the roles of advice style and source labels, and designing financial AI that supports grounded evaluation rather than simply maximizing trust.

</details>


### [2] [Understanding How Educators Configure GenAI Support for Open-Ended Learning -- An Exploratory Study of K-12 Career Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21019)
*Si Chen,Xinyue Chen,Artur Mullagaliyev,Alexander Nwanganga,Shifu Hou,Deng Pan,Ronald Metoyer,Sugana Vijay Chawla*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对15位美国K-12教育者进行访谈与设计活动，探究其如何配置生成式AI用于职业探索学习，揭示教育者在控制维度上的配置行为及面临的三类挑战，并提出系统支持与边界设定建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI虽能通过生成、个性化与学习者建模支持开放式学习，但教育者缺乏围绕教学目标来塑造这些能力的手段，因此需研究教育者如何配置GenAI。

Method: 以K-12职业探索为探索性情境，对15位美国教育者开展访谈与设计活动。

Result: 教育者不仅配置AI生成的学习体验，还配置学生活动何时被推断、学习者信息是否持久保存、谁能访问这些信息以及信息如何影响后续人工行动；同时面临三类挑战：识别超出熟悉用法的控制可能性、将通用AI分解为可理解的功能与责任、通过预期教学行动识别有用信息。

Conclusion: GenAI系统应支持教育者表达并测试配置，同时在个性化、推断、持久化、披露与行动方面设定边界，使AI支持的学习与不断变化的学习者需求保持一致。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) can support open-ended learning through generation, personalization, and learner modeling, yet educators need ways to shape these capabilities around educational goals. Through interviews and design activities with 15 U.S. educators, we examined educator configuration of GenAI using K-12 career exploration as an exploratory context. Educators configured not only AI-generated experiences, but also when student activity became an inference, whether learner information persisted, who could access it, and how it informed subsequent human action. They also faced challenges translating teaching needs into configurations: recognizing possibilities for control beyond familiar uses of GenAI, decomposing general-purpose AI into understandable functions and responsibilities, and identifying useful information through intended teaching actions. We discuss how GenAI systems can support educators in expressing and testing configurations, while establishing boundaries around personalization, inference, persistence, disclosure, and action to keep AI-supported learning aligned with evolving learner needs.

</details>


### [3] [Decoding the Dashboard: Data Comics to Support Students' Understanding of Learning Analytics Visualisations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21141)
*Mikaela Elizabeth Milesi,Vanessa Echeverria,Lixiang Yan,Yueqiao Jin,Riordan Alfredo,Jie Xiang Fan,Linxuan Zhao,Dragan Gašević,Yi-Shan Tsai,Roberto Martinez-Maldonado*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨数据漫画（data comics）如何作为辅助材料补充学习分析仪表盘（LADs），以提升学生的可视化素养与数据解读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LADs旨在帮助学生理解学习数据以支持反思与决策，但其可视化内容往往较为复杂，尤其是对可视化素养较低的学生而言难以解读。叙事技巧（如标注图表、数据漫画）虽能直接传达洞见，但尚未被用作赋权学生自主探索可视化的辅助材料。

Method: 采用定性研究，设计了一个包含可视化图表及相应数据漫画解释的多模态LAD，并对18名护理专业学生及其4名教师进行访谈。

Result: 数据分析显示，数据漫画清晰、有吸引力，能帮助复杂可视化更易理解，但需精心设计以避免信息过载。

Conclusion: 学生和教师均对数据漫画作为提升LAD可解释性的手段持接受态度。

Abstract: Learning analytics dashboards (LADs) are intended to help students make sense of their learning data to support reflection and decision-making. However, their visualisations can be complex, particularly for students with low visualisation literacy. Narrative techniques, such as annotated charts and data comics, have been used to communicate insights directly, but not as supplementary materials to empower students to explore their visualisations themselves. In response, we conducted a qualitative study examining how data comics can complement LADs. We interviewed 18 nursing students and 4 of their teachers about a multimodal LAD containing visualisations with data comics explaining them. Analysis showed that data comics were clear, engaging, and helped make complex visualisations more accessible, though they must be carefully designed to avoid overwhelming students with information. The findings suggest that both students and teachers are receptive to data comics as a means of supporting the interpretability of LADs.

</details>


### [4] [Exploring Text Classification Models with Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21142)
*Daniel Kerrigan,Brian Barr,Enrico Bertini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分析文本分类语言模型的行为，开发了SAEfarer工具，探索SAE特征与模型预测、错误之间的关系，并通过5名博士生的专家试点评估验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型日益重要，人们希望提升其透明度以更好理解内部行为。近期可解释性研究聚焦于用稀疏自编码器将某层的神经元激活分解为人类可理解的特征（每个特征代表模型学到的一个概念），但尚未充分应用于文本分类模型的行为分析。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器分析文本分类语言模型的行为，提出探索SAE特征与模型预测及错误之间关系的技术，并将这些技术集成到名为SAEfarer的工具中，用于分析文本分类语言模型学到的概念。

Result: 通过一项包含5名博士生的专家试点评估（expert pilot evaluation）对SAEfarer进行了评估。

Conclusion: SAE可用于分析文本分类语言模型的行为，SAEfarer工具帮助用户探索模型学到的概念与预测/错误的关系，并通过专家试点评估进行了初步验证。

Abstract: As language models (LMs) rise in prominence, there is interest in making them more transparent in order to better understand their internal behavior. Recent interpretability work has focused on using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to break down neuron activations at a given layer in the LM into human-understandable features, where each feature represents a concept that the model has learned. In this paper, we share work on using SAEs to analyze the behavior of text classification LMs. We present techniques for exploring the relationships between the SAE's features and the model's predictions and errors. We integrate these techniques into SAEfarer, a tool for analyzing concepts learned by text classification LMs. We assess SAEfarer in an expert pilot evaluation with five Ph.D. students.

</details>


### [5] [Human Driver Temperament and the Safety Impact of a C-V2X Denial-of-Service Flooding Attack in Mixed-Autonomy Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21206)
*Rasheed Bello,Gurcan Comert,Varghese Vaidyan,Akinbobola Jegede,Vijay Bendigeri,Judith Mwakalonge*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过OMNeT++/INET（5G NR-V2X）与SUMO联合微观仿真，考察信号交叉口SPaT拒绝服务攻击下CAV失效保护策略与周围人工驾驶者性情（temperament）的交互影响。结果表明，驾驶员性情对攻击的放大效应是具体且有限的：仅加剧激进环境下的急刹行为，对闯红灯和冲突等攻击主要特征无显著影响，但性情本身主导基线风险，形成13–18倍的谨慎—激进梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAV依赖SPaT消息通过信号交叉口，DoS洪泛攻击会阻断SPaT并迫使CAV进入失效保护模式；然而交叉口内还有人工驾驶车辆，事故后果不仅取决于攻击与CAV策略，还取决于周围人类驾驶者的行为。作者希望填补这一"人因维度"的研究空白，检验人类驾驶群体是否会系统性放大此类攻击风险，并质疑"以谨慎测试群体界定风险上限"的失效保护设计假设。

Method: 构建OMNeT++/INET（5G NR-V2X）与SUMO耦合的微观交通仿真，针对一条信号交叉口走廊；扫描CAV市场渗透率10–90%、四种经过标定的驾驶员性情（从谨慎到激进）以及两种基于标准的失效保护策略——最小风险机动（MRM）与自适应巡航控制（ACC）保持行驶回退；每个攻击实验组均与策略匹配的无攻击基线作差分对照。

Result: 性情对攻击的影响是具体而温和的，而非全面放大。激进环境仅恶化一项指标：保持行驶型失效保护对邻近驾驶者施加的急刹（p=0.03），从接近零升至+8次/1000车·秒。激进驾驶看似抑制追尾冲突，但实因洪泛清空了激进驾驶者形成的排队，属于流量改善而非安全性提升。在攻击的核心特征（CAV闯红灯与交叉冲突）上，性情无可检测影响。相反，性情主导基线风险，产生13–18倍的谨慎—激进梯度，远大于攻击本身的影响，而攻击是通过已被CAV普及堵塞的通道起作用的。

Conclusion: 人类驾驶者群体决定交叉口有多危险，但不会系统性放大该SPaT DoS攻击。因此，失效保护设计不能假定谨慎的测试群体即可界定风险上限。

Abstract: Cooperative and connected automated vehicles (CAVs) rely on Signal Phase and Timing (SPaT) messages to cross signalized intersections; a denial-of-service (DoS) flood that blocks SPaT forces CAVs into a fail-safe mode. Because human-driven vehicles share the intersection, the safety consequence depends not only on the attack and the CAV fail-safe policy, but on how the surrounding human drivers behave. We investigate this human-factors dimension with a coupled OMNeT++/INET (5G NR-V2X) and SUMO microsimulation of a signalized corridor, sweeping CAV market penetration (10-90%), four calibrated driver temperaments (cautious to aggressive) and two standards-based fail-safe policies, a minimal-risk maneuver (MRM) and an adaptive cruise control (ACC) keep-driving fallback, with each attack arm differenced against its policy-matched no-attack baseline. Temperament's effect on the attack is specific and modest rather than a blanket amplification. Aggressive surroundings worsen one metric, the hard-braking a keep-driving fail-safe forces on nearby drivers (p = 0.03), rising from near zero to +8 episodes/1000 veh-s. They appear to dampen rear-end conflicts, but only because the flood clears the queues aggressive drivers build, so the gain is in flow, not safety. On the attack's primary signatures, CAV red-light running and crossing conflicts, temperament has no detectable effect. It instead dominates baseline risk, producing a 13- to 18-fold cautious-to-aggressive gradient far larger than the attack itself, which acts through a channel already congested by CAV adoption. Human driver populations determine how dangerous the intersection is but do not systematically amplify this attack, so fail-safe design cannot assume a cautious test population bounds the risk.

</details>


### [6] [Self-Care and Mental Health: Mapping Over A Decade of HCI Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21239)
*Anna Fang,Tony Wang,Jenny Fu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该文系统分析了91篇SIGCHI心理健康自我照护论文，提出六种自我照护取向，为HCI自我照护研究提供定位词汇与设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI处于自我照护技术转向的中心，但学界缺乏对这类干预服务对象、所支持实践、技术中介方式及设计假设的系统性认识。

Method: 从ACM数字图书馆检索2015年至2026年6月的91篇SIGCHI心理健康自我照护干预论文，并进行解释性综合（interpretive synthesis）。

Result: 归纳出六种自我照护取向，揭示这些取向如何通过关于自我、照护与技术的共同假设来构成"照护"。

Conclusion: 研究提供了定位HCI心理健康自我照护的互联词汇，凸显照护责任向用户转移的趋势，并讨论了针对"一般"用户提供更具情境化自我照护技术设计的启示。

Abstract: Technology increasingly supports self-care for understanding and improving one's own mental health. HCI is at the center of the turn towards self-care technology, yet we lack an account of who these interventions serve, what practices they support, how technology mediates those practices, and assumptions underlying design for self-care. In order to characterize the current landscape and inform future research, we analyzed 91 SIGCHI papers that contribute HCI interventions for mental health self-care from the ACM Digital Library from 2015 through June 2026. Then, we conducted an interpretive synthesis to surface six orientations of self-care, which describe how HCI self-care interventions constitute care through shared assumptions regarding self, care, and technology. Overall, our work provides an interconnected vocabulary for positioning HCI mental health self-care, highlights changing responsibilities of care towards users, and discusses implications for providing a more situated account of HCI self-care technology in addressing the 'general' user.

</details>


### [7] [Two's a Crowd: Human and AI-Based Copresence for Developers with ADHD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21254)
*Veronica Pimenova,Seth Bernstein,Shalini Madan,Dhruv Jain,Venkatesh Potluri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过访谈14位患有ADHD的软件工程师，研究了他们在人-人和人-AI共处（copresence）实践中的协作方式，并据此提出面向AI工具的设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作与沟通对开发者生产力和福祉至关重要，但受注意力、内在动机、人际问责等人类因素制约。ADHD开发者尤其面临混合办公环境中的持续障碍，他们常依赖共处实践（如body doubling、结对编程）支持执行功能，而新兴的智能体AI编码助手正在重塑这些协作动态，因此需要实证研究。

Method: 对14位患有ADHD的软件工程师进行半结构化访谈，考察其人-人与人-AI共处实践的参与情况，并将发现映射到Goffman共处理论与Forsgren等人的SPACE开发者生产力框架上。

Result: 传统人-人共处提供关键的社交支持和入职引导结构，但迫使开发者不断管理职业声誉并牺牲个人隐私；而人-AI共处则帮助开发者维持问责感与认知流畅状态，同时避免人际观察带来的社交焦虑、绩效评判和监视感。

Conclusion: 基于这些实证发现，作者将开发者共处实践映射到Goffman共处理论和SPACE生产力框架的核心维度，并为促进ADHD开发者包容性协作的AI工具提出了设计建议。

Abstract: Effective collaboration and communication are vital to developer productivity and well-being, yet remain constrained by human factors such as attention, intrinsic motivation, and interpersonal accountability. These constraints are particularly vital for developers identifying with Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD), who navigate persistent environmental barriers in modern hybrid workplace settings. While developers with ADHD frequently rely on collaborative copresence practices (such as body doubling or pair programming) to support executive function, the recent emergence of agentic AI coding assistants has begun reshaping these collaborative dynamics. To investigate how developers with ADHD engage in human and AI-based copresence practices, we conducted semi-structured interviews with 14 software engineers with ADHD. Our findings reveal that while traditional human-human copresence provides critical social support and onboarding structure, it forces developers to constantly manage professional reputation and sacrifice personal privacy. Conversely, developers leverage emerging human-AI copresence to maintain accountability and cognitive flow without the social anxiety, performance judgment, or surveillance associated with human observation. Based on these empirical insights, we map developer copresence practices onto core dimensions of Goffman's copresence theory and Forsgren et al.'s SPACE framework of developer productivity, and provide design recommendations for AI-based tools that promote inclusive collaboration for developers with ADHD.

</details>


### [8] [GUIDE: Designer-in-the-loop Authoring of Conformant Generative User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21285)
*Hyewon Lee,Ziying Wang,Aiden Moy,Saran Nagubandi,Jason Wu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: GUIDE是一个GenUI开发环境，允许设计师在创建和编辑界面时持续检查并优化生成式UI的行为，通过提示优化和自适应一致性评分模型来适配设计意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式UI（GenUI）虽能按需生成界面，但设计师难以直接指定或查看每个生成的界面，导致设计意图（如品牌一致性、需求符合度）难以强制执行。因此需要一种让设计师持续参与并约束GenUI行为的系统。

Method: 提出GUIDE系统，利用设计师的修改与交互，通过提示优化（prompt optimization）以及一种新颖的自适应一致性评分模型（adaptive conformance scoring model）来适配GenUI行为。

Result: 一致性评分模型在真实与合成应用界面的留出对比中，达到或超越了专有LLM基线；在12名UI/UX从业者的研究中，参与者认为GUIDE有效且易用，并显著偏好其对齐输出，优于使用示例和模型生成design.md的强基线。

Conclusion: GUIDE能够帮助设计师持续监督和优化生成式UI，使其更符合设计意图，验证了系统与评分模型的有效性和可用性。

Abstract: Generative User Interfaces (GenUIs) enable applications to generate interfaces on demand from user needs and context. Like conventional UIs, they must still reflect designers' intent and conform to requirements such as brand identity. Unlike conventional UIs, designers cannot directly specify or see every interface a GenUI may produce, making design intent harder to enforce. We introduce GUIDE (GenUI Development Environment), a system that lets designers continuously inspect and refine GenUI behavior as they create and edit interfaces. GUIDE uses designers' modifications and interactions to adapt GenUIs through prompt optimization and a novel adaptive conformance scoring model. We validate GUIDE's scoring model and system. The scoring model matched or outperformed proprietary LLM baselines on held-out comparisons of real and synthetic application screens. In a study with 12 UI/UX practitioners, participants found GUIDE effective and usable and significantly preferred aligned outputs over a strong baseline using exemplars and a model-generated design.md.

</details>


### [9] [AESSI: An Around-Ear Silent Speech Interface for Cross-Day Online Reuse without Test-Day Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21436)
*Xiran Xu,Mochu Dong,Yujie Yan,Chenxi Wang,Yu Jiao,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AESSI——一种基于耳周电极的无声语音接口，采用掩码上下文表征预训练(MCRP)，实现了无需测试日校准、跨天可泛化的日常句子识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无声语音接口可实现无声音的私密交流，并可能帮助卒中后构音障碍患者。但日常复用要求与发音相关的表征能够跨天泛化，克服传感器重新定位与生理变化带来的影响。

Method: 提出AESSI(耳周无声语音接口)，采用掩码上下文表征预训练(MCRP)：学生网络从被掩码的时频输入预测教师网络的表征，以增强对记录变异性的鲁棒性。采集24名参与者的44段电生理记录，涵盖25个日常汉语句子；以6名参与者完整末次记录作为留出测试。

Result: 在无测试日校准条件下，AESSI平均准确率达92.24%，比最佳自适应基线高50.57个百分点。5名参与者在各自末次记录至少21天后，各完成50次独立随机在线测试且无校准，总体准确率达98.0%；CPU回放中预处理与推理时间中位数为31.70 ms。

Conclusion: 结果验证了端到端系统运行能力，支持无需测试日校准的在线复用；论文附带演示。

Abstract: Silent speech interfaces (SSIs) enable private communication without audible speech and may support people with post-stroke dysarthria. Everyday reuse requires articulation-related representations that generalize across days despite sensor repositioning and physiological changes. We present AESSI, an around-ear SSI using masked-context representation pretraining (MCRP): a student predicts teacher representations from masked time-frequency inputs to encourage robustness to recording variability. We collected 44 electrophysiological recordings from 24 participants for 25 everyday Mandarin sentences. With six participants' complete final recordings held out, AESSI achieved 92.24 percent mean accuracy without test-day calibration. AESSI exceeded the best adapted baseline by 50.57 percentage points. At least 21 days after each participant's last recording, five participants each completed 50 independently randomized online tests without calibration, achieving 98.0 percent overall accuracy. Median preprocessing and inference time in CPU replay was 31.70 ms. These results demonstrate end-to-end system operation and support online reuse without test-day calibration. Demo is included with the paper.

</details>


### [10] [People escalate against a competitor labelled human and hold back against one labelled an optimising machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21439)
*Vinicius Ferraz,Leon Houf*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过预注册实验（N=1,395）在动态全支付拍卖中区分了竞争AI时的"对手效应"与"信息效应"，发现仅告知信息即可显著影响承诺升级行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们越来越多地与AI智能体而非人类对手竞争，但"面对AI"带来的行为影响究竟来自对手本身还是信息环境，尚不清楚。区分这两条渠道对组织和政策制定具有重要意义，因为对手效应是特定计算系统的属性，而信息效应是可控的信息环境属性。

Method: 预注册实验（N=1,395），采用动态全支付拍卖这一重复竞赛任务，其中承诺升级由激励机制自然产生。实验操纵参与者被告知的对手身份（人类、模仿人类训练的AI、为竞争优化的AI），并与实际面对的对手交叉设计，且采用无欺骗设计。此外还检验了策略表述和个体差异的解释。

Result: 被告知的信息影响承诺升级：当可能面对人类时中位价格上升6.7点，当可能面对优化机器时下降8.8点，差距约为竞赛奖金的15%，且仅由信息产生。与AI竞争会降低价格，但同时也降低了双方最终均获正收益的概率，显示对手渠道的独立效应。信息效应无法由策略表述或个体差异解释，与"人类可能成为对手时激发的竞争性反应"一致。

Conclusion: 描述AI竞争对手并非行为中性。信息渠道与对手渠道具有不同后果：前者可由组织或政策制定者控制，后者则与特定计算系统相关。这为理解人机竞争中的行为机制及政策干预提供了依据。

Abstract: People increasingly compete against AI agents rather than other human opponents. We distinguish two channels: an opponent effect and an information effect. These are different elements with different consequences: the opponent effect is specific to a given computational system, the information effect a property of the information environment that an organisation or policymaker can control. We separate them in a preregistered experiment (N = 1,395) using a dynamic all-pay auction, a repeated contest in which escalation of commitment arises from the incentives. What participants are told about the opponent (human, an AI trained to imitate people, or an AI trained to compete well) is varied and crossed with who they actually face, in a deception-free design. What people are told influences escalation: the median price rises by 6.7 points when a human might be the opponent and falls by 8.8 when an optimising machine might be, a spread of about 15% of the prize value of the competition, produced by information alone. Competing against the AI agents lowers prices, yet reduces the chance that both sides finish with positive earnings, showing distinct effects of the opponent channel. The information effect is not explained by articulated strategy, or individual differences, and is consistent with a competitive response engaged when a human is a live possibility. This shows that describing an AI competitor is not behaviourally neutral.

</details>


### [11] [Do We Care About Personalization and Explainability? An Interview Study with News Recommendation Engineers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21547)
*Jasmin Kareem,Siddharth Mehrotra,Martijn C. Willemsen,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过对9家新闻机构15位技术相关人员的半结构化访谈，揭示了新闻个性化与可解释性在实践中的真实状况，并为新闻工程师与研究者提供了可操作的可解释性采用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统可解释性研究多聚焦终端用户，忽视了系统构建者与维护者（如新闻工程师）的视角及其潜在应用场景（如模型调试）。本文旨在填补这一空白，考察新闻工程师及相关技术利益相关者如何在实践中理解与实现个性化与可解释性。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，在9家新闻机构（涵盖不同地区、公共与私营部门）开展15次访谈，调查塑造其做法的挑战与动机。

Result: 个性化对新闻机构并非总是直接或可取的选择，用户追踪、编辑控制与资源限制等顾虑常限制其采用；即便在已部署个性化新闻推荐系统的机构中，可解释性也很少被优先考虑，日常运营需求常优先于长期透明目标；各机构对可解释性的定义差异很大，但部分机构展现出有价值的内部实践与可视化工具，促进了工程团队与新闻编辑室之间的沟通。

Conclusion: 基于分析，作者为新闻工程师与研究者提供了在新闻个性化流程中采用可解释性方法的实用且可操作的指南。

Abstract: Research on explainability in recommender systems largely centers on end users, overlooking the perspectives of those who build and maintain these systems and their potential use cases such as model debugging. In this study, we examine how news engineers and related technical stakeholders perceive and implement personalization and explainability in practice. We conducted 15 semi-structured interviews across nine news organizations, spanning diverse regions in both public and private sectors, to investigate the challenges and motivations shaping their approaches. Our findings reveal that personalization is not always a straightforward or desirable choice for news organizations, as concerns around user tracking, editorial control, and resource constraints often limit its adoption. Even among organizations implementing personalized news recommender systems in production, explainability is rarely prioritized, with day-to-day operational demands frequently taking precedence over longer-term transparency goals. Definitions of explainability vary widely across organizations, though some demonstrate promising internal practices and visualization tools that facilitate communication between engineering teams and newsrooms. Based on our analysis, we provide actionable and practical guidelines for news engineers and researchers on how to adopt explainability methods within a news personalization pipeline.

</details>


### [12] [Notrix: Understanding Machine Learning Solutions Across Computational Notebooks at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21775)
*Xiaotian Su,Hongxin Fu,Xiaoyu Zhang,April Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Notrix 是一个交互式可视分析工具，可将数百个计算笔记本作为一个集合进行画像：把每个单元格分类为13种机器学习阶段，得到阶段序列，并按结构而非代码聚类，配合四级粒度下始终一致的三个协调视图，帮助用户跨笔记本理解模式并定位单个笔记本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算笔记本让问题求解过程可见，但通常一次只能看一个。在 Kaggle 等数据科学平台上，一个竞赛可积累数百个笔记本。要做集合层面的分析，需要刻画跨所有笔记本反复出现的解题模式，同时又能挑出特定笔记本深入查看和学习。然而标准笔记本缺乏共同基础：工作流非线性、单元格不声明意图、相同代码可能服务不同目的，导致数百个笔记本只是彼此独立的文档。

Method: 基于一项形成性研究（N=11）的启发，Notrix 将每个单元格分类为13种机器学习阶段之一，从而把每个笔记本转化为一个阶段序列，并依据序列结构而非代码进行聚类。为在分析范围从整个集合缩小到单个单元格时保持表示一致，Notrix 设计了三个协调视图——Workflow、Structural Matrix 和 Detail——它们在全部四个粒度层级上都同时呈现。

Result: 在一项采用两个 Kaggle 集合（各含400多个笔记本）的被试内研究（N=17）中，参与者使用 Notrix 回答关于全部笔记本的问题更准确（中位数88% vs. 50%），同时每分钟打开的笔记本数量减少80%。值得注意的是，14道题中有4道参与者无需打开任何笔记本即可作答（交互日志，N=14）。参与者还报告在使用 Notrix 时心理需求、时间需求和压力显著更低（经 Holm-Bonferroni 校正）。

Conclusion: Notrix 通过将数百个笔记本统一为可按结构聚类和比较的阶段序列，并在四个粒度层级上提供一致的协调视图，使集合级分析更准确、更高效，且显著降低认知负荷。它将原本彼此孤立的数百份文档转化为一个可整体画像的集合，支持跨笔记本的模式发现与单笔记本的深入学习。

Abstract: Computational notebooks make problem-solving visible, but typically only one notebook at a time. Meanwhile, in data science platforms like Kaggle, one competition can accumulate hundreds of notebooks. Effective collection-level analysis requires characterizing recurring solution patterns across all notebooks, as well as isolating specific notebooks for closer examination and learning. However, standard notebooks provide no common basis for this. Their workflows are nonlinear, cells declare no intent, and identical code can serve different ends, leaving hundreds of notebooks as separate documents. In this paper, we present Notrix, an interactive visual analytics tool for profiling hundreds of notebooks as one collection. Inspired by a formative study (N = 11), Notrix classifies every cell into one of thirteen machine learning (ML) stages, turning each notebook into a stage sequence, and clusters those sequences by structure rather than by code. To keep the representation constant as the scope narrows from the whole collection to a single cell, Notrix features three coordinated views---Workflow, Structural Matrix, and Detail---that appear at all four levels of granularity. In a within-subject study (N = 17) using two Kaggle collections of over 400 notebooks each, we observed participants answered questions about all notebooks more accurately with Notrix (median 88% vs. 50%) while opening 80% fewer notebooks per minute. Notably, four of the fourteen answered it without opening a single notebook (interaction logs, N = 14). Participants also reported significantly lower mental demand, temporal demand, and stress with Notrix (Holm-Bonferroni adjusted).

</details>


### [13] [Comparing Hand and Controller Avatars with Hand Tracking and Controller-Based Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21799)
*Natalia Ocampo,J. Felipe Gonzalez,Robert J. Teather*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了手部追踪与控制器两种输入设备，以及手部和控制器虚拟形象两种视觉表示在目标获取任务中的影响，发现手部追踪受益于任何视觉形象，而控制器在不匹配形象下表现下降更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究提示VR输入设备与其视觉表示之间的一致性会影响用户体验与表现，但输入与虚拟形象的具体组合效应仍缺乏深入探究。

Method: 考察常见输入设备（手部追踪、控制器）与视觉表示（手部、控制器虚拟形象）的组合，涵盖抓取与捏合手势，涉及输入、形象与目标尺寸共16种组合，测量目标获取任务的表现与主观成功感。

Result: 手部追踪在有任何视觉表示（即使不匹配）时，准确率比无形象条件下最高高出5.8%。控制器总体更受偏好且任务完成更快，但不匹配形象对控制器的负面影响更强，当虚拟手势与真实动作不一致时，准确率比匹配条件下降5.6%。

Conclusion: 不准确的反馈可能比完全没有形象反馈更具干扰性；输入设备与虚拟形象的一致性对VR交互表现具有重要影响。

Abstract: Previous research suggests that the congruency between common VR input devices - such as controllers or hand tracking - and their visual representations (e.g., hand or controller avatars) influences user experience and performance. However, the specific effects of input-avatar combinations remain underexplored. We study the effects of common input devices (hand tracking and controllers) and visual representations (hand and controller avatars) on performance and perceived success in target acquisition tasks. We included both grasping and pinching gestures across 16 combinations of input, avatar, and target size. Results indicate that hand tracking benefits from any form of visual representation - even when mismatched - achieving up to 5.8% greater accuracy compared to having no avatar, likely due to its reliance on visual feedback in the absence of a physical prop. Controllers were generally preferred and offered faster task completion. However, mismatched avatars had a stronger negative effect with controllers, particularly when the virtual gesture did not align with the physical action, leading to a 5.6% drop in accuracy compared to the matched condition - suggesting that inaccurate feedback can be more disruptive than having no avatar feedback at all.

</details>


### [14] [An Agentic Just-in-Time Adaptive Intervention System for Personalized Sleep Support: Proof-of-Concept Study with N of 1 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21805)
*Nick Rezaee,Chelsea Boccagno*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种概念验证型睡眠即时自适应干预（JITAI），利用AI智能体自动审查个人数据、评估提醒、调整干预并记录决策以供人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统JITAI多依赖预定义规则和人工配置，难以灵活适应变化的情境，因此需要更自动、更自适应的方案。

Method: 在Home Assistant中按可配置时间表运行，智能体依据可复用的技能文件审查30天睡眠与行为数据（包括身体活动、智能手机使用和睡前习惯），以识别模式并创建或更新自动提醒。

Result: 初步运行验证了技术可行性，成功完成数据审查与干预决策，将提醒限制为每天三次，并保存了决策记录。

Conclusion: 智能体式AI有望实现灵活、自适应的睡眠JITAI；该架构支持未来与固定或基于规则的干预进行比较，但需要人工监督，并可扩展到其他健康行为。

Abstract: Background: Just-in-time adaptive interventions (JITAIs) can use behavioral data to adapt support to changing contexts, but many rely on predefined rules and manual configuration.
  Objective: We developed a proof-of-concept sleep JITAI using an AI agent to review personal data, evaluate reminders, adapt interventions, and record decisions for human review.
  Methods: Running in Home Assistant on a configurable schedule, the agent follows a reusable skill file to review 30 days of sleep and behavioral data, including physical activity, smartphone use, and bedtime routines, to identify patterns and create or update automated reminders.
  Results: Initial runs demonstrated technical feasibility, successfully completing data review and intervention decisions while limiting reminders to three per day and saving decision records.
  Conclusions: Agentic AI may enable flexible, adaptive sleep JITAIs. The architecture supports future comparison with fixed or rulebased interventions, requires human oversight, and could extend to other health behaviors.

</details>


### [15] [Touvigation: Embodied Adaptive Object Acquisition for Blind and Low-Vision Users in Unfamiliar Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21828)
*George Xi Wang,Xiangyu Li,Shaoyue Wen,Jiaqian Hu,Junan Xie,Yupeng Wang,Ziyue Shi,Qijun Chen,Maaike Bouwmeester,Yuhua Jin,Jing Qian*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Touvigation 是一个免手持物体获取系统，将视觉语言理解与持久化本地空间建模结合，为盲人和低视力用户提供低延迟、身体相对的自适应导航指引，任务成功率达100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲人和低视力用户在陌生室内环境中寻找并获取物体困难；现有的基于视觉语言模型的助手虽能提供语义描述，但存在延迟、幻觉，且指引与具身行动配合不佳。

Method: 基于对8位盲人和低视力参与者的形成性访谈，设计多阶段引导框架，将视觉语言理解与持久化本地空间建模相结合，随用户从定向、行走到伸手及触觉验证的转换阶段自适应调整空间参照。用12位参与者对比多模态大语言模型助手和无辅助搜索进行评估。

Result: Touvigation 任务成功率达100%，多模态助手为58%，无辅助搜索为85%；同时减少了完成时间和认知负荷。

Conclusion: 持久化空间定位与自适应具身引导能有效改善盲人和低视力用户的物体获取体验。

Abstract: Blind and low-vision users often face challenges when locating and physically acquiring objects in unfamiliar indoor environments. Existing vision-language-model-based assistants can provide semantic descriptions but may introduce latency, hallucinations, and guidance that is poorly aligned with embodied action. We present Touvigation, a hands-free object acquisition system that combines vision-language understanding with persistent local spatial modeling to provide low-latency, body-relative guidance. Drawing on formative interviews with eight blind and low-vision participants, we design a multi-stage guidance framework that adapts spatial references as users transition from orienting, to walking, to reaching and tactile verification. We evaluated Touvigation with 12 blind and low-vision participants against a multimodal large-language-model assistant and unassisted search. Touvigation achieved 100% task success, compared with 58% for the multimodal assistant and 85% for unassisted search, while reducing completion time and cognitive workload. Our findings demonstrate how persistent spatial grounding and adaptive embodied guidance can improve object acquisition for blind and low-vision users.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Counting Blessings: Tracing the Evolution of Gratitude Practices and Technology Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21853)
*Qiuyue,Zhong,Jeongah Lee,Drishti Goel,Violeta J. Rodríguez,Dong Whi Yoo,Koustuv Saha,Ravi Karkar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文通过回顾性、实物引导的访谈研究，探讨感恩实践如何随人生情境变化而改变，提出"适应性感恩实践模型"，并给出情境化支持等设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感恩技术支持人们通过反思所珍视之事来提升幸福感，但感恩在不同情境下作用不同。随着人生状况变化，人们从感恩中寻求的东西也会改变，甚至感恩本身是否恰当也会变化。现有技术如何适应并支持这种变化尚不清楚，这是研究的出发点。

Method: 对17位已实践感恩1至15年的成年人进行回顾性、实物引导（artifact-elicitation）访谈，分析参与者对自身情境的评价如何塑造其需求，并归纳出实践模式。

Result: 参与者的情境评价塑造了他们的需求，形成六种反复出现的实践模式，其中包括一个"感恩变得勉强/被迫"的边界。研究还发现，即便在同一人生情境内，评价也会发生转变；参与者会调整活动、形式与节奏，在竞争性需求或情绪未准备好时中断，并在感恩再次有用时恢复。

Conclusion: 论文提出"适应性感恩实践模型"（Adaptive Gratitude Practice Model），解释感恩实践随情境与评价的动态变化；并据此提出三方面设计启示：情境化支持、通过过往记录实现自我理解、以及随人生情境变化的关系性关怀。

Abstract: Gratitude technologies support well-being by prompting reflection on what people appreciate. But gratitude does not serve the same purpose in every circumstance: as life situations change, so does what people seek from it, and whether it feels appropriate at all. To understand how technology can adapt to and support such shifts, we conducted retrospective, artifact-elicitation interviews with 17 adults who had practiced gratitude for one to fifteen years. Participants' appraisals of their situations shaped what they needed, yielding six recurring practice patterns, including a boundary where gratitude felt forced. We contribute the Adaptive Gratitude Practice Model, which explains how appraisals shifted even within the same life situation, how participants adapted activities, modalities, and rhythms, lapsed under competing demands or emotional unreadiness, and resumed when gratitude again felt useful. Additionally, we derive design implications for situated support, self-understanding through past records, and relational care with changing life situations.

</details>


### [17] [Comparing Haptic Feedback Across Hand Tracking and Controllers in VR Object Interaction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21869)
*Natalia Ocampo,J. Felipe Gonzalez,Robert J. Teather,Kiyoshi Kiyokawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究对比VR中手部追踪与手柄交互下的振动、冲击和无反馈三种触觉条件，发现手柄在抓握和捏合上更快，手部追踪在捏合上更准且更受欢迎；触觉对性能影响有限，但振动最受偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部追踪提供自然的VR交互，但缺乏手柄固有的触觉反馈，且不同输入方式下的触觉反馈研究不足。本文旨在系统比较触觉反馈与输入设备及交互情境之间的关系。

Method: 开展被试研究，在基于手部和手柄的VR交互中比较振动、冲击和无反馈三种条件，涵盖抓握、捏合和轻拍类手势，评估绩效、工作负荷与偏好。使用自制手套和手柄安装装置提供两种反馈，力求跨输入方式的一致性。

Result: 手柄在抓握和捏合任务中更快；手部追踪在捏合上更准确且整体更受偏好。触觉对绩效影响有限，但冲击降低了手柄抓握的准确性。相比无触觉反馈，参与者更偏好振动和冲击，其中振动最受青睐。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈、输入设备与交互情境之间的关系比以往研究所示更为复杂，需要结合具体任务与设备来设计VR触觉方案。

Abstract: Hand tracking offers natural VR interaction but lacks controllers' inherent tactile feedback, while haptic feedback across input methods remains understudied. We conducted a participant study comparing vibration, impulse, and no feedback in hand- and controller-based VR interaction across grasp-, pinch-, and tap-like gestures assessing performance, workload, and preference. A custom glove and controller-mounted device provided both feedback types, aiming for consistency across input modalities. Controllers were faster for grasp and pinch, whereas hand tracking was more accurate for pinch and preferred overall. Haptics had limited performance effects, although impulse reduced grasp accuracy with controllers. Participants preferred vibration and impulse over no haptic feedback, favouring vibration overall. Our findings reveal a more nuanced relationship between haptic feedback, input device, and interaction context than suggested by previous work.

</details>


### [18] [Depressive symptoms are reflected differently across digital contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21919)
*Yajing Wang,Emilia Marchese,Talayeh Aledavood,Juhi Kulshrestha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过6个月内1146名德国成年人的月度抑郁症状评估与被动采集的手机/电脑网络行为数据，发现抑郁症状与数字行为的关联因设备与活动类型而异，强调需关注"在线做什么、在何设备做"而非仅屏幕总时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字行为有望成为反映抑郁症状的窗口，但既有数字心理健康研究结果不一致。作者认为这种不一致部分源于测量方式的局限：依赖自我报告使用、单一设备研究，以及聚合的屏幕使用时长会掩盖设备、活动类型与参与模式上的差异。

Method: 将6个月内1,146名德国成年人的月度抑郁症状评估与被动记录的手机及电脑网页浏览轨迹相结合，考察总体、认知-情感与躯体三类抑郁症状在不同设备与活动类型所定义的数字情境中的体现。

Result: 关联在不同情境间差异显著：手机上，症状越严重与更多夜间活动、更多社交媒体/通讯/娱乐使用，以及更少但更长的使用会话相关；电脑上关联较少，主要体现为对新闻、购物与成人内容参与的减少。手机上的关联主要出现在总体与认知-情感症状，而电脑上的关联集中于躯体症状。

Conclusion: 刻画抑郁症状如何反映于数字行为，需要关注人们在线做什么以及在何种设备上进行，而不仅仅关注屏幕使用总量。

Abstract: As more of everyday life takes place online, digital behavior may provide a potential window into how depressive symptoms are reflected in daily life. Yet digital mental health studies have produced mixed findings. These inconsistencies may partly reflect how digital behavior is measured: self reported use, single device studies, and aggregate screen time can obscure differences across devices, activities, and patterns of engagement. We combined monthly assessments of depressive symptoms with passively recorded mobile and desktop web traces from 1,146 adults in Germany over six months. We examined how general, cognitive-affective, and somatic depressive symptoms are reflected across digital contexts defined by device and activity type. Associations varied markedly across these contexts. On mobile, more severe symptoms were associated with more nighttime activity, greater use of social media, messaging, and entertainment, and fewer but longer sessions. On desktop, associations were fewer and largely involved reduced engagement with news, shopping, and adult content. Mobile associations primarily arose for general and cognitive-affective symptoms, whereas desktop associations were concentrated in somatic symptoms. Our findings suggest that characterizing how depressive symptoms are reflected in digital behavior requires attending to what people do online and where, not only how much screens are used.

</details>


### [19] [Can I Trust My Body? A Three-Year Autoethnography of ChatGPT's Place in My Support System for Panic Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21925)
*Dongyijie Primo Pan,Pan Hui,Mirjana Prpa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过为期三年的分析性自我民族志，研究第一作者在惊恐障碍期间使用ChatGPT的经历，揭示提问如何塑造模型角色及症状变化如何影响后续回应，并提出惊恐发作中及发作后的"轨迹级安全"框架：Fit（可用建议）、Closure（停止点）、Continuity（持续可用）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的人向大语言模型寻求心理健康支持，但对其在多年反复惊恐发作过程中的使用情况了解甚少，尤其在症状随时间变化、突然发作与反复查证等情境下的安全性问题缺乏研究。

Method: 采用为期三年的分析性自我民族志（autoethnography），以第一作者的ChatGPT使用记录为核心，结合对话内容、个人记录，以及朋友、家人和专业人士的叙述；通过叙事分析追踪提问如何塑造ChatGPT的角色，以及早期经历如何影响后续对症状的回应。

Result: 熟悉的解释能降低对躯体感受的恐惧，但症状变化会重新引发对重病的担忧；在突发惊恐时，建议往往难以遵循，部分回复反而促发更多查证行为；对话可能在症状、查证或求助行为仍在持续时结束。

Conclusion: 作者提出惊恐发作期间及之后的"轨迹级安全"（trajectory-level safety）概念，包含三个要素：Fit（可用的建议）、Closure（为反复查证和寻求安慰提供停止点）、Continuity（随时间持续可用的理解与真人支持）。

Abstract: People increasingly seek mental health support from large language models, yet little is known about their use across years of recurrent panic. We present a three-year analytic autoethnography of the first author's ChatGPT use while living with panic disorder, drawing on conversations, personal records, and accounts from friends or family members and professionals. Narrative analysis traces how my questions shaped ChatGPT's roles and how earlier experiences influenced later responses to symptoms. Familiar explanations could make sensations less frightening, while changed symptoms renewed fears of serious illness. During sudden panic, advice could be difficult to follow, and some replies prompted further checking. Conversations could end while symptoms, checking, or help-seeking continued. We propose trajectory-level safety during and after panic: usable advice (Fit), a stopping point for repeated checking and reassurance seeking (Closure), and useful understanding and human support that remain available over time (Continuity).

</details>


### [20] [Beyond Reactive Assistance: PV-Care Using Low-Density EEG and AI to Provide Proactive, Context-Aware Help for MCI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22024)
*Simon L Liu,Manish Kumar Krishne Gowda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 PV-Care，一种融合可穿戴脑电（EEG）感知与视觉环境感知的主动式 AI 辅助系统，通过 SFR-Net 识别认知状态并生成结构化提示驱动大模型，为轻度认知障碍（MCI）用户提供实时情境感知语音帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老龄人口增长带来对智能支持系统的迫切需求，尤其是轻度认知障碍（MCI）人群。传统助手系统被动等待用户指令，无法主动提供帮助，难以满足 MCI 用户的照护需要。

Method: 提出 PV-Care 方案：结合可穿戴 EEG 传感与视觉环境感知；采用新型深度网络 SFR-Net（Spatial and Frequency Refinement Network）识别 Learning、Memory Recall、Resting 等脑状态；将 EEG 认知状态识别与 AI 视觉分析结合，生成结构化“4W-UT”提示以引导大语言模型输出语音辅助。

Result: 仿真结果与用户研究验证了 SFR-Net 的高识别准确率，以及 PV-Care 情境感知辅助的有效性。

Conclusion: PV-Care 是面向 MCI 照护的一种可行且有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing elderly population gives rise to an urgent need for intelligent support systems, particularly for individuals with Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI). This paper presents PV-Care, a proactive AI-driven assistance scheme that integrates wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) sensing with visual environmental perception to provide real-time, context-aware voice assistance for MCI users. Unlike traditional assistant systems that passively wait for user commands, PV-Care actively initiates helpful interactions based on the user's detected brain states, including Learning, Memory Recall, and Resting, using a novel deep neural architecture named Spatial and Frequency Refinement Network (SFR-Net). By combining EEG-based cognitive-state recognition with AI-based visual analysis, PV-Care generates structured "4W-UT" prompts to guide the output of large language models (LLMs). Simulation results and user studies validate the high accuracy of the proposed SFR-Net and the effectiveness of PV-Care's context-aware assistance. These results indicate that PV-Care is a feasible and promising solution for MCI caring.

</details>


### [21] [Gricea: An Open Science Platform for Conversational AI Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22039)
*Nikhil Sharma,Yunlin Gong,Xinyang Cheng,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Gricea，一个开放科学平台，将对话式 AI 研究表示为可配置、可部署的研究工件，支持研究的运行、检查、分享与复用，以促进大规模、可累积的知识构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式 AI（CAI）研究需要规模化地理解人类行为并指导设计，但现有研究中系统与实验配置的报告碎片化，导致难以复现、扩展和积累知识。

Method: 基于对既有 CAI 研究的前期分析，设计并实现 Gricea 平台，将研究流程、面向参与者的系统以及对话任务行为耦合在一起，把研究表示为可配置、可部署的研究工件。随后通过复现研究与用户研究进行验证。

Result: 在复现研究中，Gricea 成功复现了 93% 符合条件的 CUI 2026 论文配置，同时指出 96% 论文存在阻碍忠实复现的缺失信息；在用户研究中，来自不同背景的研究者与实践者均能成功构建可运行的研究，回答多种开放式研究问题。

Conclusion: Gricea 通过共享研究工件支持 CAI 研究的构建、复现与扩展，借助开放科学实现累积式知识构建。

Abstract: We need studies on conversational AI (CAI) at scale to understand human behavior and shape CAI design. However, fragmented reporting of systems and study configurations hinders replication, extension, and knowledge accumulation. We present Gricea, an open-science platform representing studies as configurable, deployable research artifacts that researchers can run, inspect, share, and reuse. Informed by a formative analysis of prior CAI research, Gricea couples study procedures, participant-facing systems, and conversational task behavior in. In a replication study using Gricea, we replicated configurations 93% of eligible CUI 2026 papers; while also flagging missing information in 96% of papers that hinder faithful replication --- further motivating Gricea's need. In a user study, researchers and practitioners from diverse backgrounds successfully constructed runnable studies addressing various open-ended research questions. Together, these findings demonstrate Gricea's support for constructing, reproducing, and extending CAI studies through shared research artifacts, enabling cumulative knowledge building through open science.

</details>


### [22] [Value-Sensitive Delegation in Everyday AI Agent Use: Evidence from OpenClaw](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22067)
*Renkai Ma,Ruyuan Wan,Xuan Lu,Fan Yang,Chen Chen,Lingyao Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究基于价值敏感设计（VSD），借助LLM分析了73,093条关于使用OpenClaw的一人称Reddit帖子，编码人类价值、智能体维度、价值满足情况与用户结果，提出"价值敏感委托"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地将工作委托给自主AI智能体，但现有评估通常只衡量任务完成度，而忽视用户真正看重的价值。因此需要从用户视角出发，理解在委托过程中哪些价值被满足或未被满足。

Method: 采用价值敏感设计（Value Sensitive Design），结合LLM辅助，对73,093条关于使用OpenClaw的一人称Reddit帖子进行编码分析，每条帖子标注其人类价值、智能体维度、价值满足情况与用户结果，最终归纳出21种价值并归为六个价值组。

Result: 21种价值归为六组：自主运作（Autonomous）、可靠（Dependable）、可负担运行（Affordable Operation）、有限触及范围（Bounded Reach）、可审查性（Reviewability）与公平可及（Equitable Access）。相对于各维度在语料中的占比，价值并非聚集在智能体输出上，而是聚集在用户围绕一次运行所设定的操作条件上。在用户描述智能体交付内容时，六组中有五组价值通常被满足；而在用户描述监督智能体时，六组价值大多未被满足。

Conclusion: 作者将这一模式概念化为"价值敏感委托"（value-sensitive delegation）：支持人类价值不仅需要关注智能体完成了什么，还需关注用户围绕委托所设定的条件，包括成本、可及性与监督。

Abstract: Users increasingly delegate work to autonomous AI agents, yet evaluations typically measure task completion rather than the values users prioritize. Using Value Sensitive Design, we analyzed, with LLM assistance, 73,093 first-person Reddit posts about using OpenClaw, each for its human value, agent aspect, value fulfillment, and user outcome. The 21 values form six value groups, including Autonomous, Dependable, and Affordable Operation, Bounded Reach, Reviewability, and Equitable Access. Relative to each aspect's corpus share, values clustered not at the agent's outputs but at the operating conditions users set around a run. Values were usually met where users described what the agent delivered, in five of six groups, and mostly unmet where users described supervising it, in all six groups. We conceptualize this pattern as value-sensitive delegation. Supporting human values requires attention not only to what an agent accomplishes, but to the conditions users set around delegation, including cost, access, and oversight.

</details>


### [23] [A Sociotechnical Review of Algorithms in Health Systems: Technical, Cost, and Human-Centered Considerations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22070)
*Victoria Chui,Kelly McConvey,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过批判性分析114篇论文，考察面向医疗系统的成本感知型AI模型的开发方式，梳理其数据、方法与结果选择及其与成本和人本关切的交叉点，揭示社会技术模型设计中的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在医疗中日益普及，用于决策、风险预测和诊断等任务，但其带来的计算、组织和社会成本常未被充分考虑。现有研究多关注降低医疗成本和提升任务效率，而对模型相关成本的考量粒度不一，缺乏系统性的审视。

Method: 对114篇论文进行批判性分析，考察这些成本感知模型的数据选择、方法选择与结果选择，并分析其与成本因素和人本关切的交叉情况。

Result: 界定了模型成本及其后续维度，揭示了报告财务、计算、组织和/或社会指标的各类研究，并指出严格的社会技术模型设计存在的缺口。

Conclusion: 批判了在开发医疗系统AI模型时评估模型相关成本与可持续性问题的益处和挑战，呼吁更系统的成本考量与社会技术设计。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) applications in healthcare are becoming increasingly prevalent, to assist health systems, providers, and patients with tasks such as decision-making, risk prediction, and diagnosis. This increasing computational potential brings AI applications to the forefront of workplace decision making, often without full consideration of subsequent computational, organizational, and social costs. These applications are leveraged to reduce healthcare costs and increase efficiency of daily tasks, with model-related costs being considered at varying levels of granularity. To understand these trends, we critically analyze 114 papers to examine how cost-aware AI models have been developed for health systems. We explore the data, method, and outcome choices of these models, as well as their intersection with cost and human-centered concerns, highlighting the gaps in rigorous sociotechnical model design. From these trends, we define model costs and subsequent dimensions, presenting insight into those studies reporting financial, computational, organizational and/or social measures. Further, we critique the benefits and challenges of evaluating model-related costs and sustainability concerns when developing AI models for health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [24] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出“稀疏先验”类并定义“稀疏维度”，证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并在TV距离下给出匹配上界，表明合适先验可克服关于样本量n的维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI方法虽广泛成功，但从n个样本学习d维分布的泛化保证随维度恶化（O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})），即便已被证明是极小极大最优。作者认为这些界过于悲观，因为仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画真实应用中分布常见的结构。

Method: 作者引入稀疏先验类，将“稀疏维度”定义为分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；在k-稀疏先验下推导分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界，并在温和附加假设下针对TV距离给出（对数因子内渐近）匹配的上界；同时证明贝叶斯设定下分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验时分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))；在TV距离下得到相匹配的上界（渐近于n,k，忽略对数项）。该结论同样适用于学习采样任务。

Conclusion: 虽然k仍可能依赖维度d或内在维度，但结果表明在适当先验下学习能够克服关于样本量n的维度灾难，说明现有界因忽略分布结构而过于悲观。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [25] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个端到端商业智能（BI）评测基准 BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%，并设计工具增强的 BI-Agent 结合后训练，实现最高40个百分点的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI工作流需要用户手动完成识别相关表、数据转换、构建连接关系等繁琐准备步骤，才能回答业务问题，过程复杂且耗时。鉴于LLM在数据处理上的强大能力，作者希望研究其能否端到端地完成BI任务，免去手动准备环节。

Method: 从公开来源收集真实BI项目，从用户仪表盘中人工抽取（问题，标准答案）对，构建 BI-Bench 基准。设计工具增强的 BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为结构化数据上的子任务（搜索、连接、转换等），并编排各BI阶段专用的数据管理方法；同时开发后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，采用监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）进一步训练 BI-Agent。

Result: 即使是前沿LLM在 BI-Bench 上表现不佳，准确率低于50%。BI-Agent 在普通LLM基础上最高提升40个百分点，经后训练的 BI-Agent 再获得最高30个百分点的增益。

Conclusion: 在复杂BI工作流中，将工具增强推理与领域特定的后训练相结合至关重要，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [26] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ETA是一种端到端可训练的动态稀疏注意力架构，通过从查询表示预测上下文阈值，并在训练中乘法性抑制次阈值logit，实现接近稠密模型质量的高效长上下文解码，带来最高2.5倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，庞大KV缓存造成严重内存带宽瓶颈。稀疏注意力虽可缓解，但刚性启发式会丢弃必要上下文，导致质量下降。因此需要一种既能硬件加速解码、又不牺牲稠密模型质量的方法。

Method: 提出Elastic Threshold Attention（ETA）：从查询表示直接预测动态上下文阈值，按难度分配稠密式上下文并剪枝常规token。训练时不是删除而是乘法性抑制次阈值logit趋近零，形成平滑均匀注意力底，避免表示崩塌；该分布式概率储备消除了初始token上的局部注意力汇。推理时硬剪枝无信息KV块并吸收粗粒度GPU块选择引入的附带token。另在Triton中实现自定义解码核，用缓存几何-概率界在O(1)时间内筛选KV块；并引入离线校准算法冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B预训练ETA模型在语言建模、常识推理和长上下文检索上媲美稠密注意力，训练稀疏度约85%、激活解码密度约38%。解码核在最长512K token序列上相较FlashAttention-2实现最高2.5倍实际解码加速；离线校准再降低27%注意力计算。

Conclusion: ETA证明可训练的弹性阈值稀疏注意力能在保持稠密模型质量的同时显著加速长上下文解码，并可通过离线校准进一步降低部署开销，适用于领域特定场景。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [27] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bio-MF：一种无需预训练、潜空间无关的一步式MeanFlow框架，用于EEG条件fNIRS生成，7.0ms完成单试次合成，显著提升混合MI-BCI解码精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合MI-BCI结合EEG与fNIRS可优于纯EEG系统，但在无法配对采集时需通过EEG-to-fNIRS跨模态生成。现有方法生成慢、依赖预训练，难以满足实时MI-BCI需求；一步生成模型虽低延迟，却因去除迭代精修而损失保真度并引入非生理伪影。

Method: 提出Bio-MF：无潜空间的一步MeanFlow框架，直接进行信号空间x预测，将信号空间输出转换为MeanFlow速度监督，仅需一次网络评估完成推理。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，集成时空交互4D编码、跨模态无分类器引导以及噪声水平门控FFT正则化。

Result: Dataset 1上EEG+合成fNIRS使ACC相对纯EEG分别提升3.37（HbR）和4.15（HbO）个百分点；Dataset 2在未见64通道EEG蒙太奇下增益为2.98和2.50个百分点。RTX PRO 6000 GPU上单试次fNIRS生成耗时7.0ms，相比1000步SCDM实现857倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF在保持任务相关生成质量的同时实现快速EEG-to-fNIRS合成，可有效支撑下游混合MI解码。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [28] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了用神经网络参数化的随机微分方程（SDE）建模类星体光变曲线的历史，并提出了连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（CDM-SGD）以及对无需解HJB偏微分方程的连续时间策略梯度强化学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体亮度呈现随机变化，其光变（light curve）数据蕴含超大质量黑洞信息，但从地面巡天时间序列中建模这些变化是一个统计难题，需要更有效的随机建模与优化方法。

Method: 1）回顾将SDE与神经网络参数化相结合来应对该难题的历史思路；2）提出连续延迟记忆随机梯度下降（CDM-SGD），其依赖离散迭代过程的过去状态；3）在二维损失景观上进行仿真，并通过调节超参数与Vanilla SGD对比；4）提出一种基于连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略且无需求解HJB偏微分方程。

Result: 在二维景观仿真中，CDM-SGD相较Vanilla SGD表现出更广的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；所提连续时间策略梯度的最优性条件可退化为已有工作中的Gibbs策略。

Conclusion: 将SDE与神经网络参数化结合是建模类星体光变的有效途径；CDM-SGD通过引入延迟记忆改善优化性能；连续时间策略梯度框架为探索性策略提供了无需解HJB方程的可行方案，并与既有Gibbs策略理论一致。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [29] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究RydbergGPT的神经标度律，发现量子临界点附近的损失-数据量关系符合带损失下限的幂律，且其两点互信息统计最接近自然语言，支持"标度行为是模型-数据对的性质"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经标度律是否依赖于数据本身的统计结构，特别是临界现象中的多尺度依赖性是否与自然语言类似，从而稳定标度行为。

Method: 训练自回归Transformer模型RydbergGPT于相互作用里德堡原子阵列的量子比特投影测量数据；分析损失随训练集规模的幂律拟合；用熵归一化、有限样本校正的互信息"两点"函数比较里德堡测量与自然语料的统计结构。

Result: 临界点附近损失-数据量关系由带损失下限修正的幂律良好描述，远离临界点时该描述质量显著下降；临界两点函数统计与自然语言最接近，远离临界的量子比特构型两点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 多尺度依赖性有助于稳定的神经标度，标度行为应视为模型-数据对的性质。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [30] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了AI评审的递归反馈回路，发现用模型生成的评审去训练后续评审模型会导致评分分布压缩和语义多样性下降，称为"科学判断坍缩"，并提出TrustReviewer系统从训练期和推理期两个阶段进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地参与科学评审，既作为自动评审者也作为人类评审的助手。当模型生成的评审进入公开数据和未来训练语料后，AI评审可能变得递归：后来的评审者会学习早期模型产生的判断。作者希望研究这一反馈回路中的单步效应，并探索如何缓解由此带来的风险。

Method: 从Llama 3.1 8B出发，先在2018–2023年ICLR官方评审上微调一个评审模型，然后用系统化混合的官方评审与模型生成评审，在ICLR 2024数据上训练四个后继模型。为缓解问题，提出TrustReviewer：训练期在精选语料上对核心评审者进行单阶段训练，以减少低质量和语义退化的监督；推理期采用成对激活引导（paired activation steering），在不额外训练或专家标注的情况下进一步抑制坍缩倾向。

Result: 引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，并降低同一论文层面和语料库层面的语义多样性，作者将这一模式称为"科学判断坍缩"。TrustReviewer的两种干预手段旨在缓解残余的坍缩倾向、保持判断多样性并改善推荐对齐。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了递归评审者训练带来的具体风险，并提供了可行的干预措施，以在AI辅助的科学评审中保持判断多样性、提升推荐对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [31] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端、基于模型的强化学习算法，用于在未知环境中对给定的线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范高效合成策略。核心思路是将LTL任务的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）与环境贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）同步，并设计新的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法进行近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中满足LTL规范（如安全性、可达性）的策略合成具有挑战性。传统非贝叶斯方法在探索-利用权衡上表现不足，难以兼顾任务满足与样本效率，因此需要借助贝叶斯RL实现更优的探索与利用平衡。

Method: 1）用LDBA表示LTL任务；2）用BAMDP表示未知环境；3）将二者同步，形成同步BAMDP结构；4）提出新的BAMCP算法，在该同步结构上实现近似贝叶斯最优策略合成。

Result: 在有限与无限时域任务实验中，该方法在属性满足度和样本效率上均优于传统无模型方法。消融研究进一步证明新BAMCP算法相较经典BAMCP在LTL任务满足方面更具价值。此外，方法成功应用于“谨慎”RL，减少了训练期间的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 将LDBA与BAMDP同步并结合定制BAMCP规划，可有效实现未知环境下LTL规范的贝叶斯最优策略合成，兼顾任务满足、样本效率与训练安全性。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [32] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单框架，将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化，通过构造辅助随机序列，用现成算法得到强凸、指数凹及一般凸损失的最优动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳在线学习中，动态遗憾是衡量学习者对抗时变比较序列表现的重要指标。但强凸和指数凹损失的最优界往往需要复杂分析，作者希望用更简单统一的方法获得这些最优结果。

Method: 核心思想是对任意比较序列构造一个辅助随机序列，使其每轮无偏、方差可控且切换次数可控。结合合适的替代损失，将动态遗憾分解为对随机序列的期望切换遗憾及其可控方差，从而借助具有切换遗憾保证的现成算法推导动态遗憾界。

Result: 对强凸和指数凹损失，得到 $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ 的动态遗憾界；对一般凸损失，同一归约恢复 $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ 的动态遗憾界。其中 $T$ 为时间跨度，$P_T$ 为比较序列的路径长度。

Conclusion: 所有结果均匹配这三类损失的极小极大最优界，说明所提框架具有通用性和简洁性。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [33] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将竞争性地质解释转化为空间先验、并通过水头观测一致性进行排序的工作流，用于数据稀缺下的高后果地下决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高后果地下决策常在数据严重稀缺下做出，专家对同一地下系统可能给出相互竞争的解释，项目早期缺乏实用手段判断哪种最真实，这种不确定性往往要等到钻多口井（耗资数百万美元）后才消除。现有评估方法或依赖主观判断，或依赖早期调查中罕见的密集数据。

Method: 将竞争性地质解释转化为备选空间先验：对每种解释，用文本到图像基础模型生成1600幅地质图像集合，用单独训练的各解释专用变分自编码器提供潜表示；监督式反演网络将水头观测映射到该潜空间，冻结解码器生成图像并映射为对数导水率场；再用稳态流动模拟得到预测水头，将失配转化为高斯形式的相容性得分。

Result: 在基于Johansen组、含三种一致性递减解释的合成基准上，925个测试案例中平均水头RMSE从精确且准确解释的0.197升至准确解释的0.227、不匹配解释的0.280；应用于WIPP的Culebra白云岩段两个已发表概念模型时，修订模型相容性权重为0.991，原模型为0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流可在钻探前依据水头观测对竞争性地质解释进行客观排序，为数据稀缺条件下的地下决策提供实用、可量化的评估手段。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [34] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASGARD两阶段教师-学生框架，使基于强化学习的无人机控制能抵抗动作空间攻击，并在攻击下完成任务且能泛化到未见攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL控制器已用于无人机导航与控制，但易受动作空间攻击：攻击者在策略生成动作后、执行器执行前篡改动作指令。现有防御多针对策略输入攻击，而针对动作空间攻击的方法需在训练时重训练策略，运行时对损坏动作缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: ASGARD为两阶段教师-学生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息结合，生成动作攻击感知的隐表示，用于训练RL控制策略以及一个向执行器输出纠正动作指令的监控器。学生阶段：编码器与监控器通过监督学习从教师对应模块蒸馏训练，仅依赖无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: 在针对无人机不同动作指令的多类攻击场景中进行评估，ASGARD对动作空间攻击具有鲁棒性，能在遭受攻击情况下完成任务；并进一步发现ASGARD可泛化到未见攻击，对隐蔽性攻击也保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: ASGARD通过教师-学生蒸馏，在无需特权信息、仅用状态历史的机载条件下，为RL无人机控制提供运行时动作空间攻击防御，兼具鲁棒性与泛化性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [35] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对生理情绪识别中的时序深度学习架构进行了系统性对比研究，跨数据集、传感配置与采样频率评估了LSTM、TCN与Transformer的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理情绪识别研究通常只评估单一模型、传感配置或数据集，难以理解这些因素如何影响识别性能，缺乏系统性对比。

Method: 基于WESAD与EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集，采用被试独立的留一被试交叉验证（LOSO-CV），比较双向LSTM、TCN与Transformer在腕部、胸部及多模态配置下的表现，并研究软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率与梯度显著性。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态上取得最高准确率（99.02%±0.51%）；LSTM在EmoWear上表现最佳，唤醒度为91.80%±1.06%，效价为89.96%±0.36%。多模态传感持续优于单部位配置；4 Hz在显著降低训练成本的同时性能接近更高频率。

Conclusion: 架构的相对优劣取决于数据集特性，不存在普遍最优模型；多模态传感更有效，4 Hz是实用的采样频率选择。研究为可穿戴生理情绪识别的架构、模态与采样频率选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [36] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC-SR 用预训练 Transformer 生成多个初始表达式草图，并由这些草图引导多区域搜索，结合尺度感知常量优化与局部符号修复来联合恢复结构和常数，在多个数据集上取得最高的符号解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归目标是从观测中恢复闭式方程以获得可解释模型，但现有方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效推理：基于搜索的方法能优化表达式结构，却依赖昂贵的组合优化和随机初始化；预训练神经模型虽能几乎即时生成公式，但预测常含符号错误。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 先用预训练 Transformer 提出多个初始草图，用这些草图在若干有希望的区域初始化搜索，从而避免在庞大表达式空间中的随机起点；每个搜索过程通过尺度感知的常量优化和局部符号修复，联合恢复结构与常数。

Result: 在带与不带虚拟变量的 SRSD-Feynman 数据集以及另外六个基准上评估：MOSAIC-SR 在每个数据集上取得最高的符号解率，同时预测精度排名前二；在存在无关虚拟输入时该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习到的先验能够将搜索聚焦于有希望的方程结构，而数值优化与符号修复对公式恢复具有重要作用。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [37] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出MCR²目标在分布偏移下可能完全失效：仅优化编码率可在相关反转后导致100%预测错误，且引入IRM/REx的不变性原则也不能消除该问题，因此可靠OOD泛化需新的假设或学习原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR²作为信息论框架能学习结构化、判别性表示并启发可解释白盒架构，但作者观察到它在分布偏移下会彻底失败，因此需要研究其OOD泛化的理论极限。

Method: 通过构造精确最优的反例进行分析：构建一个完全依赖不稳定环境特征的表示，它在编码意义上达到全局最优，但在相关性反转后完全失效，即使存在完美稳定特征；并在训练与测试输入相同的情况下，证明编码质量可任意接近最优而预测误差可任意接近100%；进一步考察引入IRM与REx背后不变性原则的影响。

Result: 发现MCR²的两大OOD局限：一是仅优化MCR²可导致完全预测失败，且存在训练中无法出现的测试输入；二是在所有测试输入都可能出现于训练中时，编码质量任意接近最优而预测误差任意接近100%。直接引入IRM/REx的不变性原则也无法消除该失败，失败表示在训练环境间拥有相同的最优编码算子。

Conclusion: 共享的编码最优性并不保证稳定预测，MCR²要实现可靠的OOD保证，需引入能跨环境建立稳定预测关系的新假设或学习原则。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [38] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 测试时通信的多智能体系统在充足算力与清晰反馈下，可显著超越独立并行尝试，甚至解决单个智能体无法完成的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统很少体现科学进步依赖协作这一特点，且通信是否能帮助智能体一直存在争议，前人结果好坏参半。作者希望探究测试时通信对困难任务的影响。
